In particular, with the acceleration of the platform as an input, the robot's heading can be made to track a chosen function of time. This is sufficient to guarantee that the robot can be induced to orbit a fixed point on the platform or to locomote persistently in a desired direction.

</details>


### [111] [Energy-Efficient Multi-Robot Coverage Path Planning of Non-Convex Regions of Interests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22189)
*Sourav Raxit,Jose Fuentes,Paulo Padrao,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Md Tamjidul Hoque,Mark Kulp,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大型非凸区域（含障碍物和禁飞区）的多机器人覆盖路径规划（MRCPP）框架，通过全局感知的条带生成、最小转弯平行路径、安全缓冲区、高效mTSP求解器以及改进的可见性图等方法，显著降低能耗（3%-40%）和计算时间（一个数量级），并在真实无人飞行器和水面机器人实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于元启发式牛耕式分解的最小能量覆盖规划算法无法达到最优能效，且大多仅支持单一类型机器人平台，亟需一种能同时处理多种机器人平台、适应大范围非凸区域（含障碍物和禁飞区）的高能效多机器人覆盖路径规划框架。

Method: MRCPP通过以下五项关键技术实现：1）全局感知的条带生成；2）生成转弯最少的平行扫掠路径；3）计算安全缓冲区确保转弯安全间隙；4）高效mTSP求解器平衡负载并最小化任务时间；5）利用改进的可见性图连接不连续路径段，同时跟踪航向角并保持过渡在安全区域内。

Result: 在真实无人飞行器（AAV）和水面机器人（ASV）实验上，MRCPP相比现有最优规划器，将3台机器人团队的平均总能耗降低3%-40%，计算时间降低一个数量级，同时保持负载均衡和良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: MRCPP框架在能效、计算效率和可扩展性方面均显著优于现有方法，并已开源发布，为多机器人覆盖路径规划提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This letter presents an energy-efficient multi-robot coverage path planning (MRCPP) framework for large, nonconvex Regions of Interest (ROI) containing obstacles and no-fly zones (NFZ). Existing minimum-energy coverage planning algorithms utilize meta-heuristic boustrophedon workspace decomposition. Therefore, even with minimum energy objectives and energy consumption constraints, they cannot achieve optimal energy efficiency. Moreover, most existing frameworks support only a single type of robotic platform. MRCPP overcomes these limitations by: generating globally-informed swath generation, creating parallel sweeping paths with minimal turns, calculating safety buffers to ensure safe turning clearance, using an efficient mTSP solver to balance workloads and minimize mission time, and connecting disjoint segments via a modified visibility graph that tracks heading angles while maintaining transitions within safe regions. The efficacy of the proposed MRCPP framework is demonstrated through real-world experiments involving autonomous aerial vehicles (AAVs) and autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs). Evaluations demonstrate that the proposed MRCPP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art planners, reducing average total energy consumption by 3\% to 40\% for a team of 3 robots and computation time by an order of magnitude, while maintaining balanced workload distribution and strong scalability across increasing fleet sizes. The MRCPP framework is released as an open-source package and videos of real-world and simulated experiments are available at https://mrc-pp.github.io.

</details>


### [112] [V-STC: A Time-Efficient Multi-Vehicle Coordinated Trajectory Planning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22196)
*Pengfei Liu,Jialing Zhou,Yuezu Lv,Guanghui Wen,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出变时间步长时空走廊(V-STC)方法，通过将走廊的空间位置和时间步长均作为决策变量进行优化，在保证碰撞安全的前提下降低多自动驾驶车辆的整体时间占用，实现更高效的协同运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多自动驾驶车辆协同运动需要在保障安全的同时提升时空利用效率，现有STC方法在时间效率方面存在不足，需要更灵活的规划框架。

Method: 构建优化模型将时空走廊中立方体的空间配置和时间步长均作为决策变量，生成变时间步长时空走廊(V-STC)，再基于此为每辆车独立规划动力学可行的轨迹。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能够在保证安全的多车协同前提下，相比现有STC方法实现更节省时间的运动。

Conclusion: V-STC通过允许时空走廊的时间步长和空间位置可变，有效提升了多车协同的时间效率，是一种安全且高效的协同规划新方法。

Abstract: Coordinating the motions of multiple autonomous vehicles (AVs) requires planning frameworks that ensure safety while making efficient use of space and time. This paper presents a new approach, termed variable-time-step spatio-temporal corridor (V-STC), that enhances the temporal efficiency of multi-vehicle coordination. An optimization model is formulated to construct a V-STC for each AV, in which both the spatial configuration of the corridor cubes and their time durations are treated as decision variables. By allowing the corridor's spatial position and time step to vary, the constructed V-STC reduces the overall temporal occupancy of each AV while maintaining collision-free separation in the spatio-temporal domain. Based on the generated V-STC, a dynamically feasible trajectory is then planned independently for each AV. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed method achieves safe multi-vehicle coordination and yields more time-efficient motion compared with existing STC approaches.

</details>


### [113] [An LLM-Driven Closed-Loop Autonomous Learning Framework for Robots Facing Uncovered Tasks in Open Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22199)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向开放环境中机器人未覆盖任务的LLM驱动闭环自主学习框架，该框架通过将成功执行和观察经验转化为可复用的局部知识，减少了对重复LLM交互的依赖，实验表明该方法在重复任务执行中降低了执行时间和LLM调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在面对未覆盖任务时往往依赖重复的大语言模型交互，且成功执行或观察到的外部行为不能被自动转化为可复用的局部知识，导致效率低下和资源浪费。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的闭环自主学习框架：首先检索局部方法库判断是否存在可复用方案；若无则触发自主学习过程，LLM作为高层推理组件进行任务分析、候选模型选择、数据收集规划和执行/观察策略组织；机器人通过自我执行和主动观察进行准实时训练和调整，将验证后的结果存入局部方法库实现未来复用。

Result: 在重复任务自执行实验中，平均总执行时间从7.7772s降至6.7779s，每个任务的平均LLM调用次数从1.0降至0.2。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将执行和观察经验转化为可复用的局部能力，有效降低对未来重复外部LLM交互的依赖，提升机器人在开放环境中处理未覆盖任务的效率。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in open environments need the ability to continuously handle tasks that are not covered by predefined local methods. However, existing approaches often rely on repeated large-language-model (LLM) interaction for uncovered tasks, and even successful executions or observed successful external behaviors are not always autonomously transformed into reusable local knowledge. In this paper, we propose an LLM-driven closed-loop autonomous learning framework for robots facing uncovered tasks in open environments. The proposed framework first retrieves the local method library to determine whether a reusable solution already exists for the current task or observed event. If no suitable method is found, it triggers an autonomous learning process in which the LLM serves as a high-level reasoning component for task analysis, candidate model selection, data collection planning, and execution or observation strategy organization. The robot then learns from both self-execution and active observation, performs quasi-real-time training and adjustment, and consolidates the validated result into the local method library for future reuse. Through this recurring closed-loop process, the robot gradually converts both execution-derived and observation-derived experience into reusable local capability while reducing future dependence on repeated external LLM interaction. Results show that the proposed framework reduces execution time and LLM dependence in both repeated-task self-execution and observation-driven settings, for example reducing the average total execution time from 7.7772s to 6.7779s and the average number of LLM calls per task from 1.0 to 0.2 in the repeated-task self-execution experiments.

</details>


### [114] [Learning-augmented robotic automation for real-world manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22235)
*Yunho Kim,Quan Nguyen,Taewhan Kim,Youngjin Heo,Joonho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习增强的机器人自动化系统（LARA），将学习型任务控制器与神经3D安全监控器集成到传统工业流程中。在电动马达生产线上部署，仅用不到20分钟的真实数据训练，系统连续运行5小时10分钟，生产108台电机，通过率99.4%，无需物理围栏，实现了接近人类节拍时间并降低了质量波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人广泛使用，但大多数操作依赖固定的路径点脚本，对环境变化脆弱。学习型控制虽然更具适应性，但尚不清楚能否在真实生产线上持续可靠运行数小时、保持稳定质量并在人员周围安全作业。

Method: 提出Learning-Augmented Robotic Automation (LARA)混合系统，整合学习型任务控制器与神经3D安全监控器，融入传统工业工作流程。采用小样本真实数据（<20分钟）训练，在电动马达生产线上执行可变形线缆插入和焊接任务。

Result: 系统连续运行5小时10分钟，生产108台电机，产品级质检通过率99.4%，无需物理围栏。保持接近人类节拍时间，同时降低了焊点质量和循环时间的变异性。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了将学习型方法扩展工业自动化的实用路径，证明了基于学习的方法能够在真实制造约束下实现长时间、高质量、安全可靠的自动化生产。

Abstract: Industrial robots are widely used in manufacturing, yet most manipulation still depends on fixed waypoint scripts that are brittle to environmental changes. Learning-based control offers a more adaptive alternative, but it remains unclear whether such methods, still mostly confined to laboratory demonstrations, can sustain hours of reliable operation, deliver consistent quality, and behave safely around people on a live production line. Here we present Learning-Augmented Robotic Automation, a hybrid system that integrates learned task controllers and a neural 3D safety monitor into conventional industrial workflows. We deployed the system on an electric-motor production line to automate deformable cable insertion and soldering under real manufacturing constraints, a step previously performed manually by human workers. With less than 20 min of real-world data per task, the system operated continuously for 5 h 10 min, producing 108 motors without physical fencing and achieving a 99.4% pass rate on product-level quality-control tests. It maintained near-human takt time while reducing variability in solder-joint quality and cycle time. These results establish a practical pathway for extending industrial automation with learning-based methods.

</details>


### [115] [CodeGraphVLP: Code-as-Planner Meets Semantic-Graph State for Non-Markovian Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22238)
*Khoa Vo,Sieu Tran,Taisei Hanyu,Yuki Ikebe,Duy Nguyen,Bui Duy Quoc Nghi,Minh Vu,Anthony Gunderman,Chase Rainwater,Anh Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeGraphVLP，一种结合持久化语义图状态、可执行代码规划器和进度引导视觉语言提示的分层框架，用于解决非马尔可夫长时域机器人操作任务，相比强VLA基线显著提升任务完成率并降低规划延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型通常假设最新观测足以进行动作推理，但在非马尔可夫长时域任务中，任务相关信息可能被遮挡或仅出现在轨迹早期，且杂乱环境和干扰物导致细粒度视觉定位脆弱，因此需要一种能处理部分可观测性和长期依赖的框架。

Method: 1) 持久化语义图：维护任务相关实体及其关系，支持部分可观测条件下的状态追踪；2) 可执行代码规划器：基于语义图执行高效进度检查，输出子任务指令和子任务相关物体；3) 进度引导的视觉语言提示：利用规划器输出构建去噪观测，引导VLA执行器聚焦关键证据。

Result: 在真实世界非马尔可夫任务上，CodeGraphVLP相比强VLA基线及其历史增强变体提升了任务完成率，同时相比VLM-in-the-loop规划方法大幅降低了规划延迟。消融实验证实了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: 通过显式维护语义图状态并利用代码规划器进行结构化推理，可以弥补纯端到端VLA模型在长时域依赖和部分可观测性上的不足，为实际机器人操作提供更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise generalist robot manipulation, but are typically trained and deployed as short-horizon policies that assume the latest observation is sufficient for action reasoning. This assumption breaks in non-Markovian long-horizon tasks, where task-relevant evidence can be occluded or appear only earlier in the trajectory, and where clutter and distractors make fine-grained visual grounding brittle. We present CodeGraphVLP, a hierarchical framework that enables reliable long-horizon manipulation by combining a persistent semantic-graph state with an executable code-based planner and progress-guided visual-language prompting. The semantic-graph maintains task-relevant entities and relations under partial observability. The synthesized planner executes over this semantic-graph to perform efficient progress checks and outputs a subtask instruction together with subtask-relevant objects. We use these outputs to construct clutter-suppressed observations that focus the VLA executor on critical evidence. On real-world non-Markovian tasks, CodeGraphVLP improves task completion over strong VLA baselines and history-enabled variants while substantially lowering planning latency compared to VLM-in-the-loop planning. We also conduct extensive ablation studies to confirm the contributions of each component.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Control Policies to Provably Satisfy Hard Affine Constraints for Black-Box Hybrid Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22244)
*Aayushi Shrivastava,Kartik Nagpal,Sairam Jinkala,Jean-Baptiste Bouvier,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对黑箱混合动力系统的安全性问题，提出了一种学习强化学习策略的方法，通过使策略在约束边界附近呈仿射且排斥的形式，并引入复位前排斥区域处理状态跳跃，从而在闭环中可证明地满足仿射状态约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱混合动力系统存在瞬时状态跳跃和未知非线性动力学，现有方法（如CBF、可达性分析、安全RL）要么依赖已知动力学，要么仅通过奖励塑造弱化违规行为，缺乏可证明的安全保证。

Method: 迫使RL策略在约束边界附近为仿射且排斥形式（针对未知非线性动力学），并针对混合系统的复位映射引入第二个复位前排斥仿射区域，防止复位后状态违反约束；推导了闭环安全约束满足的充分条件。

Result: 在约束摆和拍球两个混合动力系统环境中，该方法与最先进的奖励塑造和学习CBF方法相比，学习了更高质量的策略，同时始终满足安全约束。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在无需已知动力学模型的情况下，为黑箱混合动力系统提供可证明的安全保证，且实际性能优于现有方法。

Abstract: Ensuring safety for black-box hybrid dynamical systems presents significant challenges due to their instantaneous state jumps and unknown explicit nonlinear dynamics. Existing solutions for strict safety constraint satisfaction, like control barrier functions (CBFs) and reachability analysis, rely on direct knowledge of the dynamics. Similarly, safe reinforcement learning (RL) approaches often rely on known system dynamics or merely discourage safety violations through reward shaping. In this work, we want to learn RL policies which provably satisfy affine state constraints in closed loop for black-box hybrid dynamical systems with affine reset maps. Our key insight is forcing the RL policy to be affine and repulsive near the constraint boundaries for the unknown nonlinear dynamics of the system, providing guarantees that the trajectories will not violate the constraint. We further account for constraint violation due to instantaneous state jumps that occur due to impacts or reset maps in the hybrid system by introducing a second repulsive affine region before the reset that prevents post-reset states from violating the constraint. We derive sufficient conditions under which these policies satisfy safety constraints in closed loop. We also compare our approach with state-of-the-art reward shaping and learned-CBF methods on hybrid dynamical systems like the constrained pendulum and paddle juggler environments. In both scenarios, we show that our methodology learns higher quality policies while always satisfying the safety constraints.

</details>


### [117] [False Feasibility in Variable Impedance MPC for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22251)
*Vishal Ramesh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了变阻抗模型预测控制中，由于忽略一阶执行器动力学，参数可行集Fparam与物理可实现集Freal之间存在系统性不匹配。通过无量纲参数α=ω_sT（执行器带宽×任务时间尺度）量化该偏差，推导了1D跳跃单足运动中的解析阈值α_crit，并通过数值实验验证了偏差随α减小的单调增长规律（log-log R²=0.99）。进一步识别出第二阈值α_infeas，低于该阈值时限制刚度范围无法修复可实现性。将刚度增广为预测状态可从结构上闭合该不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变阻抗MPC将关节刚度视为瞬时决策变量，其可行集严格大于一阶执行器动力学下的物理可实现集，现有工作常将此视为建模近似（modeling approximation），而本文指出这属于公式化错误（formulation error）。作者旨在严格区分参数可行集与可实现集，并量化两者不匹配的机制与后果。

Method: 1）理论形式化：定义参数可行集Fparam与可实现集Freal，引入无量纲参数α=ω_sT；2）解析推导：针对1D跳跃单足运动，基于任务物理闭式导出阈值α_crit；3）数值验证：10组参数组合验证log-log R²=0.99；4）机制迁移：扩展到平面SLIP动力学，分析质心与支撑时间偏差；5）第二阈值识别：α_infeas<α_crit，低于此阈值限制刚度范围无效；6）修复方案：将刚度增广至预测状态。

Result: 1）在α低于α_crit时，无任何刚度指令可实现参数集预测；2）偏差随α减小单调增长，预测标度与数值实验高度吻合（R²=0.99）；3）在平面SLIP中，主要后果为质心轨迹和支撑时间偏差，摩擦效应为三级可观测量；4）存在α_infeas阈值，低于该阈值保守调参无法修复可实现性；5）将刚度增广为预测状态可从结构上消除不匹配。

Conclusion: 变阻抗MPC中将刚度作为瞬时决策变量是一个公式化错误而非建模近似，其后果在低执行器带宽或快任务时间尺度下尤为严重。本文的解析阈值和α参数化框架为判断何时该错误显著提供了理论依据，并指出状态增广是结构性的修复方案。

Abstract: Variable impedance model predictive control (MPC) formulations that treat joint stiffness as an instantaneous decision variable operate on a feasible set strictly larger than the physically realizable set under first-order actuator dynamics. We identify this as a formulation error rather than a modeling approximation, formalize the distinction between the parameter-based feasible set Fparam and the realizable set Freal, and characterize the regime of mismatch via the dimensionless parameter alpha = omega_sT (actuator bandwidth times task timescale). For the 1D hopping monoped, we prove that below an analytical threshold alpha_crit derived in closed form from task physics, no admissible stiffness command realizes the parameter-based prediction. Numerical validation in 1D shows monotonic deviation growth as alpha decreases, with the predicted scaling holding across ten parameter combinations (log-log R2 = 0.99). Mechanism transfer to planar spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics confirms center-of-mass and stance-timing deviation as the primary consequence, with regime-dependent friction effects as a tertiary observable. A second threshold alpha_infeas < alpha_crit establishes a floor below which restricting the admissible stiffness range cannot repair realizability, closing the conservative-tuning objection on structural grounds. Augmenting the prediction state with stiffness closes the mismatch by construction.

</details>


### [118] [A Kinematic Analysis of Palm Degrees of Freedom for Enhancing Thumb Opposability in Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22283)
*HyoJae Kang,Yeong Jae Park,Hyunmok Jung,Joonho Lee,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过定义基于体素化 fingertip 可达区域的 overlap workspace volume，量化分析了手掌自由度（DoF）对五指机械手拇指对掌性的增强作用。结果表明，手掌 DoF 通过 repositioning 手指基部位置显著提升对掌性（尤其对无名指和小指），但在总 DoF 受限时，将 DoF 从手指重新分配到手掌会导致 overlap workspace 扩展与运动学冗余之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对机械手手掌自由度在拇指-手指交互中量化作用的理解，尤其缺乏不依赖物体或接触模型的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含五指（拇指5 DoF，其他手指3-4 DoF）的手部运动学模型，在相邻手指间引入手掌运动；定义基于体素化 fingertip 可达区域的 overlap workspace volume 作为指标；对比七种配置（包括增加总 DoF 和保持总 DoF 不变但在手指与手掌间重新分配 DoF）。

Result: 手掌 DoF 显著提升对掌性，尤其对无名指和小指，其机制是 repositioning 基部位置而非延长可达范围。总 DoF 约束下，DoF 重新分配至手掌会导致 overlap workspace 扩展与运动学冗余间的 trade-off。

Conclusion: 手掌 DoF 与手指 DoF 在手部运动学中扮演不同角色，应联合考虑；本研究提供了一个不依赖物体或接触模型的定量框架，为融入手掌运动的机械手设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the kinematic role of palm degrees of freedom (DoF) in enhancing thumb opposability in a five-finger robotic hand. A hand model consisting of a five DoF thumb and four fingers with three to four DoF is analyzed, where palm motion is introduced between adjacent fingers. To quantitatively evaluate thumb-finger interaction, the overlap workspace volume is defined based on voxelized fingertip reachable regions. Seven cases are considered, including configurations with increased total DoF and configurations in which the total DoF is maintained by redistributing DoF from the fingers to the palm. The results show that palm DoF significantly improves opposability, particularly for the ring and little fingers, by repositioning their base locations rather than simply extending their reachable range. However, when the total DoF is constrained, redistributing DoF to the palm leads to trade-offs between overlap workspace expansion and kinematic redundancy. These findings indicate that palm DoF and finger DoF play distinct roles in hand kinematics and should be considered jointly in design. This study provides a quantitative framework for evaluating palm-induced opposability without relying on object or contact models and offers practical design guidelines for incorporating palm motion in robotic hands.

</details>


### [119] [LeHome: A Simulation Environment for Deformable Object Manipulation in Household Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22363)
*Zeyi Li,Yushi Yang,Shawn Xie,Kyle Xu,Tianxing Chen,Yuran Wang,Zhenhao Shen,Yan Shen,Yue Chen,Wenjun Li,Yukun Zheng,Chaorui Zhang,Siyi Lin,Fei Teng,Hongjun Yang,Ming Chen,Steve Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeHome是一个面向家庭场景的变形物体操作仿真环境，支持高保真动力学模拟、多种机器人形态（尤其低成本机器人），填补了现有仿真器在变形物体模拟和实际硬件部署之间的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境是机器人最具挑战性的应用场景之一，而变形物体操作因种类繁多、动力学复杂以及现有仿真器缺乏可靠支持而尤为困难，亟需一个高保真且实用的仿真平台来推动研究。

Method: 构建LeHome仿真环境，覆盖服装、食品等多种变形物体类别，实现高保真动力学与真实交互；支持多种机器人形态，重点兼容低成本机器人，可在资源受限硬件上进行端到端任务评估。

Result: LeHome在变形物体模拟保真度和交互真实性上优于现有仿真器，并为低成本机器人上的家庭任务提供了可扩展的测试平台。

Conclusion: LeHome弥合了变形物体仿真与实际机器人平台之间的差距，为推进家庭机器人研究提供了可扩展的试验平台。

Abstract: Household environments present one of the most common, impactful yet challenging application domains for robotics. Within household scenarios, manipulating deformable objects is particularly difficult, both in simulation and real-world execution, due to varied categories and shapes, complex dynamics, and diverse material properties, as well as the lack of reliable deformable-object support in existing simulations. We introduce LeHome, a comprehensive simulation environment designed for deformable object manipulation in household scenarios. LeHome covers a wide spectrum of deformable objects, such as garments and food items, offering high-fidelity dynamics and realistic interactions that existing simulators struggle to simulate accurately. Moreover, LeHome supports multiple robotic embodiments and emphasizes low-cost robots as a core focus, enabling end-to-end evaluation of household tasks on resource-constrained hardware. By bridging the gap between realistic deformable object simulation and practical robotic platforms, LeHome provides a scalable testbed for advancing household robotics. Webpage: https://lehome-web.github.io/ .

</details>


### [120] [Adaptive vs. Static Robot-to-Human Handover: A Study on Orientation and Approach Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22378)
*Federico Biagi,Dario Onfiani,Simone Silenzi,Cristina Iani,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人手姿态和下游任务的动态物体递送框架，通过AI手部姿态估计与运动学约束轨迹实现人机交接。用户研究表明该方法相比静态基线显著降低了认知负荷和生理压力，提升了信任度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人-人交接策略多为静态开环或仅调整位置的方法，未考虑人类如何抓取物体，迫使人类用户去适应机器人，缺乏对人机交互中人体工效学和认知负荷的优化。

Method: 集成基于AI的手部姿态估计与运动学约束的平滑轨迹，实时动态调整物体的递送姿态（包括位置和朝向），使机器人根据用户手部姿态和下游任务目标自适应调整交接策略。

Result: 相比静态基线，动态对齐方法显著降低了用户的认知负荷（NASA-TLX）和生理压力（眨眼率），同时提高了主观信任度（Human-Robot Trust Scale）。

Conclusion: 任务感知和姿态感知的动态交接系统能够实现更流畅、更符合人体工效的人机协作，为下一代人机交互系统设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Robot-to-human handovers often rely on static, open-loop strategies (or, at best, approaches that adapt only the position), which generally do not consider how the object will be grasped by the human, thus requiring the user to adapt. This work presents a novel adaptive framework that dynamically adjusts the object's delivery pose in real time based on the user's hand pose and the intended downstream task. By integrating AI-based hand pose estimation with smooth, kinematically constrained trajectories, the system ensures a safe approach and an optimal handover orientation. A comprehensive user study compares the proposed adaptive approach against a static baseline across multiple tasks, evaluating both subjective metrics (NASA-TLX, Human-Robot Trust Scale) and objective physiological data (blink rate measured via wearable eye-trackers). The results demonstrate that dynamic alignment significantly reduces users' cognitive workload and physiological stress, while increasing perceived trust in the robot's reliability. These findings highlight the potential of task- and pose-aware systems for enabling fluid and ergonomic human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [121] [QDTraj: Exploration of Diverse Trajectory Primitives for Articulated Objects Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22551)
*Mathilde Kappel,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Louis Annabi,Faiz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QDTraj方法，基于质量-多样性算法生成多样化的机器人轨迹基元，使机器人能够在开放环境中操控多种铰接物体，在仿真和真实世界中均验证了其高效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人在开放环境中自主执行操作任务仍面临挑战，尤其是在操控铰接物体时，现有方法缺乏对多种解决方案的考虑，导致机器人无法适应真实环境中的动态约束和意外变化。

Method: 基于质量-多样性（Quality-Diversity）算法，利用稀疏奖励探索，自动生成一组多样化且高性能的轨迹基元，涵盖不同铰接物体（如铰链和滑块）的低层轨迹原语。

Result: QDTraj在铰链和滑块激活任务中生成的多样化轨迹数量至少是其他方法的5倍；在PartNetMobility数据集的30种铰接物体上平均生成704条不同轨迹，泛化性良好。

Conclusion: QDTraj通过生成多样化的轨迹基元，有效提升了机器人在开放环境中操控铰接物体的能力，并且代码已开源。

Abstract: Thanks to the latest advances in learning and robotics, domestic robots are beginning to enter homes, aiming to execute household chores autonomously. However, robots still struggle to perform autonomous manipulation tasks in open-ended environments. In this context, this paper presents a method that enables a robot to manipulate a wide spectrum of articulated objects.
  In this paper, we automatically generate different robot low-level trajectory primitives to manipulate given object articulations. A very important point when it comes to generating expert trajectories is to consider the diversity of solutions to achieve the same goal. Indeed, knowing diverse low-level primitives to accomplish the same task enables the robot to choose the optimal solution in its real-world environment, with live constraints and unexpected changes. To do so, we propose a method based on Quality-Diversity algorithms that leverages sparse reward exploration in order to generate a set of diverse and high-performing trajectory primitives for a given manipulation task.
  We validated our method, QDTraj, by generating diverse trajectories in simulation and deploying them in the real world. QDTraj generates at least 5 times more diverse trajectories for both hinge and slider activation tasks, outperforming the other methods we compared against. We assessed the generalization of our method over 30 articulations of the PartNetMobility articulated object dataset, with an average of 704 different trajectories by task. Code is publicly available at: https://kappel.web.isir.upmc.fr/trajectory_primitive_website

</details>


### [122] [RedVLA: Physical Red Teaming for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22591)
*Yuhao Zhang,Borong Zhang,Jiaming Fan,Jiachen Shen,Yishuai Cai,Yaodong Yang,Jiaming Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RedVLA，首个针对VLA模型物理安全的红队测试框架，通过风险场景合成与风险放大两阶段流程，系统性地揭露不安全行为，在6个代表性VLA模型上实现高达95.5%的攻击成功率。同时提出基于RedVLA生成数据的轻量级安全防护SimpleVLA-Guard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在真实世界部署中存在不可预测且不可逆的物理安全风险，但缺乏有效的机制来主动检测这些物理安全风险。

Method: 两阶段流程：(I) 风险场景合成——从良性轨迹中识别关键交互区域，将风险因子置于这些区域以纠缠VLA执行流并引发目标不安全行为；(II) 风险放大——通过梯度自由优化结合轨迹特征迭代优化风险因子状态，确保跨异构模型的稳定触发。

Result: 在六个代表性VLA模型上，RedVLA揭示了多种不安全行为，在10次优化迭代内攻击成功率最高达95.5%。

Conclusion: RedVLA能有效检测VLA模型的物理安全风险，基于其生成数据构建的SimpleVLA-Guard可提供轻量级安全防护。

Abstract: The real-world deployment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains limited by the risk of unpredictable and irreversible physical harm. However, we currently lack effective mechanisms to proactively detect these physical safety risks before deployment. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{RedVLA}, the first red teaming framework for physical safety in VLA models. We systematically uncover unsafe behaviors through a two-stage process: (I) \textbf{Risk Scenario Synthesis} constructs a valid and task-feasible initial risk scene. Specifically, it identifies critical interaction regions from benign trajectories and positions the risk factor within these regions, aiming to entangle it with the VLA's execution flow and elicit a target unsafe behavior. (II) \textbf{Risk Amplification} ensures stable elicitation across heterogeneous models. It iteratively refines the risk factor state through gradient-free optimization guided by trajectory features. Experiments on six representative VLA models show that RedVLA uncovers diverse unsafe behaviors and achieves the ASR up to 95.5\% within 10 optimization iterations. To mitigate these risks, we further propose SimpleVLA-Guard, a lightweight safety guard built from RedVLA-generated data. Our data, assets, and code are available \href{https://redvla.github.io}{here}.

</details>
